created: '2026-05-28'
description: Censys is an internet intelligence and attack surface management platform that continuously scans the public IPv4 space, IPv6 announced ranges, and the global certificate transparency ecosystem to produce a comprehensive public dataset of internet-connected hosts, services, certificates, and web properties. The Censys Platform exposes this data through a unified REST API supporting host/web/certificate search, collections, threat hunting, adversary investigation, asset graph traversal, and supply-chain intelligence — making it a primary alternative to Shodan for security researchers, SOC teams, threat hunters, and attack-surface management programs.

features:
- description: Continuously refreshed host, service, certificate, and web-property dataset spanning the public IPv4 space, announced IPv6 ranges, and the global Certificate Transparency log ecosystem.
  name: Global Internet Asset Index
- description: Unified, expressive query language used across hosts, certificates, and web properties — replaces the legacy Censys Search Language (CSL).
  name: Censys Query Language (CenQL)
- description: Saved asset groupings that can be queried, aggregated, and monitored with events, webhooks, and email notifications.
  name: Collections
- description: Automated pivot engine that hunts adversary infrastructure across host endpoints, fingerprints, certificates, and threats with on-demand discovery scans.
  name: Threat Hunting (CensEye)
- description: Certificate-to-host and host-to-endpoint pivots, value-count enrichment, and live re-scan endpoints purpose-built for investigating malicious infrastructure.
  name: Adversary Investigation
- description: Graph-based attack surface composition — define seeds, build graphs, page through assets/edges, manage exclusions, and read per-asset risks.
  name: Asset Graph
- description: Track third-party supplier exposure and supply-chain risk against the Censys asset dataset.
  name: Supply Chain Intelligence
- description: Organization, membership, invitation, audit-log, credit, and per-user credit-usage APIs for tenant administration.
  name: Account Management
- description: First-class annotation surface for tagging and commenting on assets across the platform.
  name: Tags and Comments
- description: Official connectors and community tooling for the major SOC / threat-intel ecosystems.
  name: Integrations (Splunk, SOAR, Google SecOps, Maltego, recon-ng, nmap)
- description: Censys Search v1/v2 and Censys ASM APIs remain available during the migration window; ASM endpoints are not deprecated.
  name: Legacy Search and ASM Compatibility
finops:
- name: Censys Finops
  service_category: Security Intelligence
  slug: censys-finops
image: https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/9468155?s=200&v=4
integrations:
- description: Censys Splunk Add-on and Apps for SIEM enrichment and dashboards.
  name: Splunk
- description: Censys SOAR connector for automated response playbooks.
  name: Splunk SOAR
- description: Censys Google SecOps dashboard for SOC operations on Chronicle.
  name: Google SecOps (Chronicle)
- description: Documented integration path for ingesting Censys data into Sentinel.
  name: Microsoft Sentinel
- description: Censys Maltego transforms for graph-based investigations.
  name: Maltego
- description: Censys modules for the recon-ng reconnaissance framework.
  name: recon-ng
- description: NSE script that leverages Censys data for passive recon.
  name: nmap
- description: Censys Unified Cloud Connector pulls cloud-native asset inventory into Censys ASM.
  name: AWS / Azure / GCP
- description: ASM ticketing/workflow integrations for asset and risk remediation tracking.
  name: Jira and ServiceNow
- description: Censys-published Postman collections for Search and ASM APIs.
  name: Postman

layout: provider

name: Censys
nav: Providers
network: true
overview: 'Censys publishes 14 APIs on the [APIs.io](https://apis.io/) network, including Account Management API, Adversary Investigation API, Asset Graphs API, and 11 more. Tagged areas include Security, Internet Intelligence, Attack Surface Management, Threat Hunting, and cyber-threat-intelligence.


  The Censys catalog on APIs.io includes 3 JSON-LD contexts and 2 Spectral governance rulesets.


  Censys'' developer surface includes authentication, developer portal, signup flow, pricing, engineering blog, support, CLI, and 25 more developer resources.'

It is our backlog EXCEPT where this provider already did the work: catalog_earned is how much of the class was satisfied at all, and catalog_earned_first_party how much of that came from artifacts the provider published rather than ones we generated (roadmap#221). catalog_earned_first_party is a FLOOR, not the whole share: only ~40 of the rubric''s 113 checks carry a provenance class at all, so a check we cannot attribute counts toward neither side. - description: Continuous external attack-surface monitoring with risk scoring, integrations, and remediation workflows.
  name: Censys Attack Surface Management (ASM)
- description: Advanced pivoting, CensEye automation, Censys Threat Dataset, and on-demand scanning for proactive infrastructure hunting.
  name: Censys Threat Hunting
- description: Original search.censys.io interface — Search v1/v2 APIs being migrated to the Platform API.
  name: Censys Search (Legacy)
tags:
- Security
- Internet Intelligence
- Attack Surface Management
- Threat Hunting
- cyber-threat-intelligence
- OSINT
- Internet Scanning
- Certificates

use_cases:
- description: Discover unknown assets, monitor exposure, and prioritize risk across an organization's internet-facing footprint.
  name: External Attack Surface Management
- description: Pivot across certificates, services, and endpoints to track adversary infrastructure with CensEye-driven discovery scans.
  name: Threat Hunting
- description: Investigate IPs, certificates, and hosts associated with known malicious activity; rescan suspect infrastructure on demand.
  name: Adversary Investigation
- description: Enrich alerts in Splunk / SOAR / Google SecOps with Censys host, service, and certificate context.
  name: SOC Triage and Enrichment
- description: Identify exposed services, deprecated TLS versions, and known-vulnerable software footprints at internet scale.
  name: Vulnerability Discovery
- description: Search the certificate-transparency-backed corpus for organizational PKI inventory and rogue/expired cert detection.
  name: Certificate Inventory
- description: Enumerate subdomains and related assets via certificate, DNS, and IP-graph pivots.
  name: Subdomain and Asset Discovery
- description: Track supplier exposure and weak links across third-party internet-facing assets.
  name: Supply Chain Risk
- description: Long-running Censys research mission supporting peer-reviewed internet-scale measurement studies.
  name: Academic and Internet Measurement Research
website: https://censys.com
